R_C_REL_F_P / R_C_REL_F_M / R_C_REL_S_P / R_C_REL_S_M / R_C_TEMP_F_P / R_C_TEMP_F_M / R_C_TEMP_S_P / R_C_TEMP_S_M / R_W_REL_F_P / R_W_REL_F_M / R_W_REL_S_P / R_W_REL_S_M / R_W_TEMP_F_P / R_W_TEMP_F_M / R_W_TEMP_S_P / R_W_TEMP_S_M / T_C_REL_F_P / T_C_REL_F_M / T_C_REL_S_P / T_C_REL_S_M / T_C_TEMP_F_P / T_C_TEMP_F_M / T_C_TEMP_S_P / T_C_TEMP_S_M / T_W_REL_F_P / T_W_REL_F_M / T_W_REL_S_P / T_W_REL_S_M / T_W_TEMP_F_P / T_W_TEMP_F_M / T_W_TEMP_S_P / T_W_TEMP_S_M: ⟦sosar⟧ dieses? ⟦qokey⟧ ⟦okeolan⟧ wenn? ⟦qol⟧ und? dieser Stoff? ⟦qor⟧ ⟦aram⟧ nach? ⟦lkan⟧
